[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 1/60]
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge

         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];

      end

            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe

               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),
               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));
         end
      end
   endgenerate

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

... (showing lines 118-282 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule


[ASSISTANT]
I've reviewed the starting point and testbench. Key insights:

1. **Baseline architecture**: 64 PEs, each with an 8×8 signed multiplier + 32-bit accumulator, activations propagate east through registers, psums flow south, with input skew (i cycles/row) + output de-skew (N-1-j/columns).
2. **Accumulator width can be cut to 19 bits**: max |Σ of 8 products| = 8·128·128 = 2^17, which fits in 19-bit signed — then sign-extend to 32 at the output. This shrinks all 64 adders and the de-skew registers.
3. **Broadcast architecture**: If each row's activation is *broadcast* to all 8 columns (instead of propagating east through 8 registers), and row i is delayed by i+7 cycles, all columns finish simultaneously → de-skew network disappears entirely (saves ~500+ flops).
4. **Carry-save accumulation**: keeping psums in redundant (sum, carry) form removes the carry-propagate adder from the per-cycle loop.

Let me first evaluate the unmodified starting point for a cost baseline, while creating my v2 design (19-bit accumulators + broadcast):
  -> run_evaluation(filename='starting_point.v')
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU style).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Architecture notes (vs. original):\n//  * Internal accumulator width PW=19: the exact sum of eight 8x8 signed\n//    products is bounded by 8*2^14 = 2^17, which fits in 19-bit two's\n//    complement. Results are sign-extended to 32 bits at the output, so the\n//    32-bit contract is preserved while every adder shrinks.\n//  * Each row's activation is BROADCAST to all columns; row i is delayed by\n//    i+K (K=7) compute cycles. PE(i,j) at compute cycle t multiplies\n//    A[t-i-K][i] * B[i][j]; the psum chain then delivers dot(A[t-8-K],B) at\n//    the bottom of every column simultaneously at t = m+15 = 2N-1 after row m\n//    was accepted. No eastward activation registers, no output de-skew.\n//  * Weights load exactly as the original: a per-column southward shift\n//    chain fed by w_data; cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*].\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // internal accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t// compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_row,      // broadcast activation for this row\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_row) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t psum_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en)\n\t   psum_south <= psum_north + {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;   // 15 compute cycles injection-to-output\n   localparam PW  = 19;\t // exact accumulator width\n   localparam K   = 7;\t // uniform input delay aligning all columns\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed by (i + K) compute cycles, zeros injected while\n   // a_valid is low. The delayed row is broadcast to all N columns.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_row [0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t localparam DEPTH = i + K;\n\t reg [DW-1:0] sh [0:DEPTH-1];\n\t integer k;\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t    if (rst) begin\n\t       for (k = 0; k < DEPTH; k = k + 1)\n\t\t sh[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t    end\n\t    else if (en) begin\n\t       sh[0] <= din;\n\t       for (k = 1; k < DEPTH; k = k + 1)\n\t\t sh[k] <= sh[k-1];\n\t    end\n\t end\n\t assign a_row[i] = sh[DEPTH-1];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Weight-stationary array: psum flows south; activations are broadcast.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign psum_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n\t assign w_v[0][j]    = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk\t     (clk),\n\t       .rst\t     (rst),\n\t       .ld\t     (ld),\n\t       .en\t     (en),\n\t       .w_north\t     (w_v[i][j]),\n\t       .a_row\t     (a_row[i]),\n\t       .psum_north   (psum_v[i][j]),\n\t       .w_south\t     (w_v[i+1][j]),\n\t       .psum_south   (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output: all columns are time-aligned; sign-extend PW -> ACCW.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar jj;\n   generate\n      for (jj = 0; jj < N; jj = jj + 1) begin : g_out\n\t assign c_data[jj*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t   {{(ACCW-PW){psum_v[N][jj][PW-1]}}, psum_v[N][jj]};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v2.v')

[ASSISTANT]
The failure is an off-by-one in my skew derivation. Let me redo it carefully for the broadcast architecture (no activation registers between columns):

- Bottom psum at compute time `t`: `S(8,j,t) = Σ_i prod_ij(t-(7-i))` (row i's term added `7-i` cycles before emerging)
- `prod_ij(τ) = A[τ-D_i][i]·B[i][j]` with row delay `D_i`
- Requiring all terms to be row `m`: `t-7+i-D_i = m ∀i` ⇒ `D_i = i + (t-7-m)` ⇒ **`D_i = i + N`** (i.e. K = 8, not 7), giving result row `m` at `t = m+15` ✓
  -> edit_file(filename='design_v2.v', new_string='   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;   // 15 compute cycles injection-to-output\n   localparam PW  = 19;\t // exact accumulator width\n   localparam K   = N;\t // uniform input delay aligning all columns\n\t\t\t // D_i = i + N: row i emerges from the psum chain\n\t\t\t // (7-i) cycles after its product is formed, so the\n\t\t\t // bottom psum at compute time t is exactly\n\t\t\t // dot(A[t-2N+1], B) = C[t-15][*].', old_string='   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;   // 15 compute cycles injection-to-output\n   localparam PW  = 19;\t // exact accumulator width\n   localparam K   = 7;\t // uniform input delay aligning all columns')

[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the failure completely. The vectors at lines 260-267 are a **mid-drain weight reload**, and results drain across it (lines 268+). The original forms product (m,i,j) at en-position `p(m)+i+j` — so in-flight rows mix OLD weights (formed before the load) and NEW weights (formed after) along the anti-diagonal `i+j`. My broadcast architecture forms products at `p(m)+7+i` (independent of j) — a different mixing pattern. **The product formation timing `p(m)+i+j` is contractual**, which forces the east-propagating structure.

So my optimization levers are:
1. **Narrow the internal accumulator to 19 bits** (max |Σ 8 products| = 8·2^14 = 2^17 fits in 19-bit signed; sign-extend to 32 at the output) — shrinks 64 adders, psum flops (2048→1216), de-skew flops (896→532).
2. **Carry-save psum chain** — replaces the per-PE carry-propagate adder with a 3:2 compressor; the single CPA moves to the column exit (once per column, not in the accumulate loop).

Let me build both variants:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU style).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Architecture: identical timing skeleton to the original (per-row input\n// skew, west->east activation propagation, north->south psum chain with one\n// multiply-accumulate per PE, per-column output de-skew, 15-cycle valid\n// pipeline), which fixes the exact per-PE product formation time\n// (p(m)+i+j in compute cycles) and hence the exact weight-mixing behavior\n// across mid-drain weight reloads.\n//\n// Optimization #1: the internal accumulator width is PW=19 instead of 32.\n// The exact sum of eight 8x8 signed products is bounded by 8*2^14 = 2^17,\n// which fits in 19-bit two's complement, so the 19-bit accumulation is\n// exact. Results are sign-extended to 32 bits at the very output.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t// compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  psum_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t psum_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east     <= a_west;\n\t    psum_south <= psum_north +\n\t\t\t  {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]\tc_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0]   psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles. Zero is\n   // injected when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up, then sign-extended to the 32-bit contract.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v3.v')
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU style).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Timing skeleton identical to the original (per-row input skew, west->east\n// activation propagation, north->south psum chain, per-column output\n// de-skew, 15-cycle valid pipeline), which fixes the exact per-PE product\n// formation time (p(m)+i+j in compute cycles) and hence the exact\n// weight-mixing behavior across mid-drain weight reloads.\n//\n// Optimization #1: internal accumulator width PW=19 (exact: |sum of eight\n// 8x8 signed products| <= 8*2^14 = 2^17 < 2^18); sign-extended to 32 at the\n// output.\n//\n// Optimization #2: the psum chain is carried in REDUNDANT (carry-save) form:\n// each PE replaces its carry-propagate add with a single 3:2 compressor\n// (carry-save adder) layer, so the per-cycle path is multiply -> CSA ->\n// register. The only carry-propagate add happens once per column, at the\n// chain exit. The pair (s,c) satisfies s + c = exact sum (mod 2^PW), and the\n// exact sum always fits in PW bits signed, so the exit CPA reproduces the\n// original 32-bit result exactly after sign extension.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t// compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  s_north,   // carry-save psum in (sum part)\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  c_north,   // carry-save psum in (carry part)\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  s_south,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [PW-1:0]   p    = {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // 3:2 compressor: {c_out, s_out} = s_north + c_north + p (mod 2^PW),\n   // where c_out is the carry vector shifted left by one position.\n   wire [PW-1:0] ssum = s_north ^ c_north ^ p;\n   wire [PW-1:0] maj  = (s_north & c_north) | (s_north & p) | (c_north & p);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w       <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t a_east  <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t s_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t c_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east  <= a_west;\n\t    s_south <= ssum;\n\t    c_south <= {maj[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]\tc_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0]   s_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [PW-1:0]   c_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // single carry-propagate add at the chain exit\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] = s_v[N][j] + c_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n              (.clk       (clk),\n               .rst       (rst),\n               .ld        (ld),\n               .en        (en),\n               .w_north   (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west    (a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north   (s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north   (c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south   (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east    (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south   (s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south   (c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles. Zero is\n   // injected when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up, then sign-extended to the 32-bit contract.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v4.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v4 passes at **cost 2,890,849** (area 4342, delay 666) — the carry-save chain helped a lot. The critical path is now `w_reg → multiplier → CSA → s_reg` (~500ps of logic). The yosys-inferred signed multiplier is the bottleneck.

My next move: **replace the inferred multiplier + separate CSA with one monolithic carry-save tree per PE**. Decompose the signed product into positive-weight operands (no internal CPA at all):
- `prod = a_u·w_u − 256·a7·w_u − 256·w7·a_u + 2^16·a7·w7` (exact identity)
- Negative terms via `−(x<<8) ≡ ~(x<<8) + 1 (mod 2^19)`, with the `+1` folded as gated constant bits
- All product operands + the incoming carry-save psum (s,c) go into a single 6-stage 3:2 compressor tree → registered (s,c) pair. Congruence `s+c ≡ true sum (mod 2^19)` is preserved; the exact final sum (≤ 2^17) is recovered by one CPA at the column exit.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU style).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Timing skeleton identical to the original (per-row input skew, west->east\n// activation propagation, north->south psum chain, per-column output\n// de-skew, 15-cycle valid pipeline), which fixes the exact per-PE product\n// formation time (p(m)+i+j in compute cycles) and hence the exact\n// weight-mixing behavior across mid-drain weight reloads.\n//\n// Optimization #1: internal accumulator width PW=19 (exact: the sum of eight\n// 8x8 signed products lies in [-2^17, 2^17]); sign-extended to 32 at the\n// output.\n//\n// Optimization #2: the psum chain is carried in REDUNDANT (carry-save) form.\n//\n// Optimization #3: the signed 8x8 product is never carry-propagated. Using\n// the exact identity (a_u = a's bit pattern, a7 = its MSB):\n//     a*w = a_u*w_u - 256*a7*w_u - 256*w7*a_u + 65536*a7*w7\n// and mod 2^PW:  -(x<<8) = (~(x<<8)) + 1, the product becomes a set of\n// positive-weight operands:\n//     pp0..pp7 : w_u[k] * a_u << k          (8 shifted AND planes)\n//     t2 + 1_a : a7 & (~(w_u<<8)) + a7      (= -256*a7*w_u)\n//     t3 + 1_w : w7 & (~(a_u<<8)) + w7      (= -256*w7*a_u)\n//     t4       : 2^16 * a7 * w7\n// together with the incoming psum pair (sin, cin) these 15 operands enter ONE\n// column-compressed 3:2 carry-save tree (6 stages), whose registered output\n// (s,c) satisfies s + c = sin + cin + a*w (mod 2^PW). The exact running sum\n// always fits in PW bits signed, so the single carry-propagate add at each\n// column exit reproduces the original 32-bit result exactly.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19,\t // accumulator shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t// compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  s_north,   // carry-save psum in (sum part)\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  c_north,   // carry-save psum in (carry part)\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  s_south,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // row 0 starts a fresh accumulation\n   wire [PW-1:0] sin = (ROW == 0) ? {PW{1'b0}} : s_north;\n   wire [PW-1:0] cin = (ROW == 0) ? [PW-1:0] one_a = {{(PW-1){1'b0}}, a7};\n   wire [PW-1:0] t3 = w7 ? {3'b111, ~a_west, {DW{1'b1}}} : {PW{1'b0}};\n   wire [PW-1:0] one_w = {{(PW-1){1'b0}}, w7};\n   wire [PW-1:0] t4 = {{(PW-17){1'b0}}, output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles. Zero is\n   // injected when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up, then sign-extended to the 32-bit contract.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v5.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v5 improved delay (590ps) but the 13 generic full-width CSA stages cost too much area (5203) → worse cost than v4. Key realization: with the sign-extended-activation identity, I can build the product from just **11 operands** (7 wide rows `w_j·(a<<j)` with free sign-extension mod 2^19, one negated row, one correction bit, plus the carry-save psum pair) — reducible in only **5 CSA levels with 9 compressors** instead of 13. Let me implement this:
  -> create_file('{"content":"//#############################################################################\\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU style).\\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\\n//\\n// Timing skeleton identical to the original (per-row input skew, west->east\\n// activation propagation, north->south psum chain, per-column output\\n// de-skew, 15-cycle valid pipeline), which fixes the exact per-PE product\\n// formation time (p(m)+i+j in compute cycles) and hence the exact\\n// weight-mixing behavior across mid-drain weight reloads.\\n//\\n// Arithmetic: the psum chain is carried in REDUNDANT (carry-save) form\\n// (s,c) with s + c = exact sum (mod 2^PW), PW = 19 (the exact sum of eight\\n// 8x8 signed products fits in 19-bit signed; sign-extended to 32 at the\\n// output). parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles. Zero is\n   // injected when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up, then sign-extended to the 32-bit contract.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
v6's failure taught me the padding was wrong (and I dropped a term). Rather than patch it, I'll implement a better-verified scheme: **radix-4 Booth recoding of the stationary weight** — only 4 narrow partial rows + a pre-registered sign-correction constant + the registered `(2^11−w)` row, giving a 9-operand / 4-level carry-save tree. I verified the identities numerically (a=−1,w=5; a=−128,w=−128; a=127,w=−1; a=−128,w=127 all check out):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU style).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Timing skeleton identical to the original (per-row input skew, west->east\n// activation propagation, north->south psum chain, per-column output\n// de-skew, 15-cycle valid pipeline), which fixes the exact per-PE product\n// formation time (p(m)+i+j in compute cycles) and hence the exact\n// weight-mixing behavior across mid-drain weight reloads.\n//\n// Optimization #1: internal accumulator width PW=19 (exact: the sum of eight\n// 8x8 signed products lies in [-2^17, 2^17]); sign-extended to 32 at the\n// output.\n//\n// Optimization #2: the psum chain is carried in REDUNDANT (carry-save) form:\n// per PE a single level of 3:2 compression, with one carry-propagate add at\n// each column exit.\n//\n// Optimization #3: the signed 8x8 product is formed by radix-4 (Booth)\n// recoding of the STATIONARY weight, so no carry-propagating multiplier\n// exists anywhere in the loop:\n//   w = sum_k d_k * 2^(2k),  d_k in {-2,-1,0,1,2}  (exact for signed w,\n//   with the implicit sign bit w[8] = w[7]).\n//   a*w = a_u*w - 256*a7*w   (a = a_u - 256*a7, a_u = unsigned bit pattern)\n//       = sum_k d_k*a_u*2^(2k)  +  a7*(2^19 - 256*w_u)   (mod 2^19)\n//   The a7 term is a7 * {negw, 8'b0} with negw = (2^11 - w_u) mod 2^11\n//   (registered at weight load), plus the single 2^16*a7*w7 correction bit.\n//   Each Booth row is a 10-bit field at position 2k: X = |d_k|*a_u (shifted\n//   by d_k's MSB), complemented when d_k < 0; the per-row complement\n//   constant ((2^2k - 2^(2k+10))*neg_k) is pre-summed at weight load into\n//   the registered constant Creg. So the tree has 9 operands and 4 levels.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19,\t // accumulator width (signed)\n    parameter ROW = 0)\t // row index (row 0 has no incoming psum)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t// compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  s_north,   // carry-save psum in (sum part)\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  c_north,   // carry-save psum in (carry part)\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  s_south,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   reg [10:0]   negw;      // (2^11 - w_u) mod 2^11, registered at load\n   reg [3:0]    negr;      // Booth sign digits, registered at load\n   reg [3:0]    mag1r;     // |d_k| != 0\n   reg [3:0]    mag2r;     // |d_k| == 2\n   reg [PW-1:0] Creg;      // summed complement constants, registered at load\n\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // row 0 starts a fresh accumulation\n   wire [PW-1:0] sin = (ROW == 0) ? {PW{1'b0}} : s_north;\n   wire [PW-1:0] cin = (ROW == 0) ? {PW{1'b0}} : c_north;\n\n   wire a7 = a_west[DW-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_u = a_west;\n\n   //#########################################################################\n   // Booth digits of the incoming weight (load path), k = 0..3\n   // d_k = -2*w[2k+1] + w[2k] + w[2k-1]  (w[8] := w[7], w[-1] := 0)\n   // mag1 = y^z, mag2 = ~mag1 & (x^y), neg = x & ~(y & z)  with x,y,z =\n   // w[2k+1], w[2k], w[2k-1]\n   //#########################################################################\n   wire [3:0] l_m1, l_m2, l_ng;\n   assign l_m1[0] = w_north[0];\n   assign l_m1[1] = w_north[2] ^ w_north[1];\n   assign l_m1[2] = w_north[4] ^ w_north[3];\n   assign l_m1[3] = w_north[6] ^ w_north[5];\n   assign l_m2[0] = ~l_m1[0] & (w_north[1] ^ w_north[0]);\n   assign l_m2[1] = ~l_m1[1] & (w_north[3] ^ w_north[2]);\n   assign l_m2[2] = ~l_m1[2] & (w_north[5] ^ w_north[4]);\n   assign l_m2[3] = ~l_m1[3] & (w_north[7] ^ w_north[6]);\n   assign l_ng[0] = w_north[1] & ~w_north[0];\n   assign l_ng[1] = w_north[3] & ~(w_north[2] & w_north[1]);\n   assign l_ng[2] = w_north[5] & ~(w_north[4] & w_north[3]);\n   assign l_ng[3] = w_north[7] & ~(w_north[6] & w_north[5]);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w      <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t negw   <= 11'b0;\n\t negr   <= 4'b0;\n\t mag1r  <= 4'b0;\n\t mag2r  <= 4'b0;\n\t Creg   <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (ld) begin\n\t w     <= w_north;\n\t negw  <= 11'd0 - w_north;   // (2^11 - w_u) mod 2^11\n\t negr  <= l_ng;\n\t mag1r <= l_m1;\n\t mag2r <= l_m2;\n\t Creg  <= (l_ng[0] ? {negw, 8'b0}\n   wire [PW-1:0] N1 = a7 ? {negw, 8'b0} : {PW{1'b0}};\n   // 2^16 * a7 * w7\n   wire [PW-1:0] T4 = {{(PW-17){1'b0}}, a7 & w[DW-1], 16'b0};\n\n   //#########################################################################\n   // 9-operand carry-save reduction: 4 levels, 7 compressors\n   //#########################################################################\n   wire [PW-1:0] a1s = R0 ^ R1 ^ Creg;\n   wire [PW-1:0] a1c = {(R0 & R1) | (R0 & Creg) | (R1 & Creg), 1'b0};\n   wire | (a2c & a3c) | (a3s & a3c), 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] c1s = b1s ^ b1c ^ b2s;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c1c = {(b1s & b1c) | (b1s & b2s) | (b1c & b2s), 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] os = c1s ^ c1c ^ b2c;\n [ASSISTANT]
v9's failure taught me two things: my k=0 sign decoder was wrong (`neg[0]` should be simply `w[1]`), and I dropped the ±2 Booth rows (`Xk` must be `2a` when `mag2=1`). Rather than patch incrementally, I worked through the full radix-4 Booth algebra by hand and verified it on several weights (w=127, −128, −1, 45):

**Product = Σ_k d_k·a_u·2^2k − 256·a7·w**, realized as 8 CSA-tree operands:
- 4 Booth rows `Rk = negr ? ~Zk : Zk` (full-word complement ⇒ correction is exactly −1 per negative row)
- `corr3 = Σ negr_k` (3-bit registered sum — the −1's)
- `N1 = a7 ? {negw,8'b0} : 0` with `negw = (2^11 − w_signed) mod 2^11` (folds both the −256·a7·w term *and* the 2^16·a7·w7 term — verified: w=−128 → N1 = 32768 = −256·(−128) ✓)
- plus the carry-save psum pair (s,c)

That's 8 operands → **4 CSA levels, 6 compressors**, no carry propagation in the loop, tiny load-time logic (digit decoders + one 11-bit negate):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU style).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Timing skeleton identical to the original (per-row input skew, west->east\n// activation propagation, north->south psum chain, per-column output\n// de-skew, 15-compute-cycle latency), which fixes the exact per-PE product\n// formation time (p(m)+i+j in compute cycles) and hence the exact\n// weight-mixing behavior across mid-drain weight reloads.\n//\n// Optimization #1: internal accumulator width PW=19 (exact: the sum of eight\n// 8x8 signed products lies in [-2^17, 2^17]); sign-extended to 32 at the\n// output.\n//\n// Optimization #2: the psum chain is carried in REDUNDANT (carry-save) form:\n// every CSA level preserves s + c = true sum (mod 2^PW), and the single\n// carry-propagate add happens at each column exit.\n//\n// Optimization #3: the signed 8x8 product is formed by radix-4 Booth\n// recoding of the STATIONARY weight (registered at load time), so no\n// carry-propagating multiplier exists anywhere in the compute loop:\n//   w = sum_k d_k*2^(2k), d_k in {-2,-1,0,1,2}  (w[8] := w[7], w[-1] := 0)\n//   a*w = (a_u - 256*a7)*w = sum_k d_k*a_u*2^(2k) - 256*a7*w\n// Each Booth row is a 10-bit field |d_k|*a_u placed at bit 2k, complemented\n// (full 19-bit word) when d_k < 0; a full-word complement contributes exactly\n// -1 (mod 2^PW), so the per-row corrections collapse into the 3-bit\n// registered sum corr3 = sum_k ng_k. The -256*a7*w term is a7 * {negw, 8'b0}\n// with negw = (2^11 - w) mod 2^11 (sign-extended w), which also absorbs the\n// 2^16*a7*w7 piece since -256*a7*w = a7*(2^19 - 256*w) mod 2^19 for the\n// SIGNED w. Total: 8 tree operands -> 4 CSA levels, 6 compressors.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t a_east  <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t s_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t c_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n\t a_east  <= a_west;\n\t cycles.
