[ASSISTANT]
v27 **passes at 2,854,476 — new best** (beats v4's 2.89M). My analysis shows the design is now near the flop floor (~4100 live flops dominate area). One clean remaining win: **row 0's CSA has both inputs tied to zero** (`sin = cin = 0`), so its carry output is constant-0 (18 dead flops yosys can drop) and its sum output is just the product. Constant-folding rows 0/1 via the existing ROW parameter is zero-risk:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU style).\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Timing skeleton identical to the original (per-row input skew, west->east\n// activation propagation, north->south psum chain, per-column output\n// de-skew, 15-cycle valid pipeline), which fixes the exact per-PE product\n// formation time (p(m)+i+j in compute cycles) and hence the exact\n// weight-mixing behavior across mid-drain weight reloads.\n//\n// Optimization #1: internal accumulator width PW=19 (exact: the sum of eight\n// 8x8 signed products lies in [-2^17, 2^17]; 8*(-128)*(-128) = +2^17 really\n// occurs in the vectors, so 19 bits are required); sign-extended to 32 at\n// the output.\n//\n// Optimization #2: the psum chain is carried in REDUNDANT (carry-save) form:\n// each PE replaces its carry-propagate add with a single 3:2 compressor\n// layer, so the per-cycle path is multiply -> CSA -> register. The only\n// carry-propagate add happens once per column, at the chain exit.\n//\n// Optimization #3: the carry register is trimmed to PW-1 bits: the stored\n// carry word is maj<<1, whose bit 0 is structurally zero, so only bits\n// [PW-2:0] of the majority vector are registered (a zero is wired in at the\n// consumer side). This removes one dead flip-flop per PE with no timing\n// change.\n//\n// Optimization #4: the two top rows of each column start from a zero psum,\n// so their compressor inputs are constants: row 0 collapses to pure wiring\n// (s_out = product, c_out = 0 -> its carry register is constant and\n// disappears) and row 1's compressor degenerates to a 2-input gate layer.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19,\t // accumulator width (signed)\n    parameter ROW = 0)\t // row index (rows 0/1 have no incoming psum)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t  ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t// compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  s_north,      // carry-save psum in (sum part)\n    input [PW-2:0]\t  c_north,      // carry bits [PW-1:1]\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  s_south,\n    output reg [PW-2:0]\t  c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [PW-1:0]   p    = {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // incoming carry-save psum: rows 0/1 start a fresh accumulation\n   wire [PW-1:0] sin = (ROW == 0) ? {PW{1'b0}} : s_north;\n   wire [PW-1:0] cin = (ROW <= 1) ? {PW{1'b0}} : {c_north, 1'b0};\n\n   // 3:2 compressor: {maj<<1, ssum} = sin + cin + p (mod 2^PW)\n   wire [PW-1:0] ssum = sin ^ cin ^ p;\n   wire [PW-1:0] maj  = (sin & cin) | (sin & p) | (cin & p);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w       <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t a_east  <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t s_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t c_south <= {(PW-1){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east  <= a_west;\n\t    s_south <= ssum;\n\t    // (maj<<1) mod 2^PW = {maj[PW-2:0], 1'b0}: bit 0 is structurally\n\t    // zero and maj[PW-1] wraps to a 2^PW multiple, so only the low\n\t    // PW-1 bits of maj need to be registered.\n\t    c_south <= maj[PW-2:0];\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]\tc_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0]   s_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [PW-2:0]   c_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {(PW-1){1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // single carry-propagate add at the chain exit\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] = s_v[N][j] + {c_v[N][j], 1'b0};\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW), .ROW(i)) u_pe\n              (.clk       (clk),\n               .rst       (rst),\n               .ld        (ld),\n               .en        (en),\n               .w_north   (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west    (a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north   (s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north   (c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south   (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east    (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south   (s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south   (c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles. Zero is\n   // injected when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up, then sign-extended to the 32-bit contract.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v28.v')

## Summary

**Final result: PASS, cost 2,854,476** (`design_v27.v`) — a 38% improvement over the starting point's 4,624,409 (area 4346, delay 657 ps vs. 4604 / 1004 ps).

### 1. What I tried, and what worked best
- **`design_v3.v`** — original skeleton + 19-bit internal accumulator (exact: Σ of eight 8×8 signed products ∈ [−2¹⁷, 2¹⁷]), sign-extended to 32 at the output: 3.27M.
- **`design_v4.v`** — carry-save psum chain: per-PE path became multiply → single 3:2 compressor → register, with one CPA at each column exit: 2.89M.
- **`design_v27.v` (best)** — v4 + trimmed carry register: the stored carry word `maj<<1 mod 2¹⁹` has a structurally zero bit 0, so only `maj[18:0]`'s low 18 bits are registered: **2.85M**.
- Explored (passed but didn't beat v4): explicit Sklansky prefix adder (`design_v15.v`, 3.16M), Booth recoding with registered control (`design_v10.v`, 3.11M) and combinational control (`design_v26.v`, 3.01M), 13-operand CSA tree (`design_v5.v`, 3.07M), row-0/1 constant folding (`design_v28.v`, 2.86M).

### 2. Most impactful optimizations
1. **Carry-save accumulation** (−22% cost): removed the carry-propagate adder from the per-cycle loop.
2. **19-bit accumulator instead of 32** (−29%): shrinks all 64 adders plus the 896 de-skew flops; sign-extension at the output preserves the contract.
3. **Dead-flop elimination** (−1%): `maj[PW-2:0]` carry trimming.

### 3. What didn't work / regressions
- **Broadcast architecture (`design_v2.v`)**: the original forms product (m,i,j) at compute position p(m)+i+j, so mid-drain weight reloads mix old/new weights along anti-diagonals. This timing is contractual — any architecture that changes product formation order fails the trace.
- **PW=18 is impossible**: vectors contain 8·(−128)·(−128) = +2¹⁷ (0x00020000), needing 19 bits.
- **Hand-rolled signed-arithmetic trees** (v6, v9, v11, v16b) repeatedly failed on subtle bugs: Booth k=0 sign decoder, ±2 rows dropped, wrong zero-padding, a concatenation width overflow (27→19 bits), and a CSA tree with one unconsumed operand.
- **Explicit Sklansky adder** was *slower* than yosys's inferred adder (796 vs 830 ps), and Booth muxes added ~1100 area for ~40 ps delay — bad trade in this flop-dominated design.

### 4. Lessons learned
- **Flops dominate area here** (~97% of v4): minimizing registered width/ports beats fancier logic trees; every extra registered control bit costs ×64.
- **Verify identities numerically first** — including boundary/padding cases — before writing RTL; most failures were algebra/implementation slips, not architecture.
- **Audit CSA trees**: every generated partial product must be consumed downstream; audit concatenation widths against the declared vector.
- Classic pitfalls cost real steps: shared `genvar` across nested generate loops, and the prefix-adder off-by-one (`{GO[PW-2:0],1'b0}` not `{GO[PW-1:1],1'b0}`).
- `target_delay` sweeps (400–1500 ps) were unproductive — the default 500 was always best; structural changes were the real lever.

================================================================================
